-          <blockquote class="cita">"Una plataforma de pòdcasts pensada per escoltar millor."</blockquote>
-          <p class="lead">ECOPOD és un projecte iniciat el 29 de setembre amb l'objectiu de desenvolupar una plataforma de pòdcasts útil, atractiva i fàcil d'utilitzar.</p>
-          <p>La proposta neix de la voluntat de crear una experiència d'usuari més clara i més agradable, evitant interfícies carregades i posant el contingut al centre.</p>
-          <p>A més de la navegació principal, el projecte inclou eines complementàries com un generador de codis QR, una calculadora i un reproductor de música personal, que aporten interacció i valor afegit a la web.</p>
-          <p>Per desenvolupar-lo, ens hem inspirat en plataformes de referència com YouTube Music, Apple Podcasts i Podverse, analitzant-ne els punts forts i les possibles millores per adaptar-ne les idees al nostre projecte.</p>
-          <div class="meta-final">
-            <div><strong>Objectiu</strong>Plataforma de pòdcasts clara i útil</div>
-            <div><strong>Inspiració</strong>YouTube Music, Apple Podcasts i Podverse</div>
-            <div><strong>Eines</strong>QR, calculadora i reproductor</div>
-          </div>

+        <div class="projecte-hero">

+        <div class="projecte-wrap">
+          <blockquote class="cita">"Una plataforma de pòdcasts pensada per escoltar millor."</blockquote>
+          <p class="lead">ECOPOD és un projecte iniciat el 29 de setembre amb l'objectiu de desenvolupar una plataforma de pòdcasts útil, atractiva i fàcil d'utilitzar.</p>
+          <p>La proposta neix de la voluntat de crear una experiència d'usuari més clara i més agradable, evitant interfícies carregades i posant el contingut al centre.</p>
+          <p>A més de la navegació principal, el projecte inclou eines complementàries com un generador de codis QR, una calculadora i un reproductor de música personal, que aporten interacció i valor afegit a la web.</p>
+          <p>Per desenvolupar-lo, ens hem inspirat en plataformes de referència com YouTube Music, Apple Podcasts i Podverse, analitzant-ne els punts forts i les possibles millores per adaptar-ne les idees al nostre projecte.</p>
+          <div class="meta-final">
+            <div><strong>Objectiu</strong>Plataforma de pòdcasts clara i útil</div>
+            <div><strong>Inspiració</strong>YouTube Music, Apple Podcasts i Podverse</div>
+            <div><strong>Eines</strong>QR, calculadora i reproductor</div>
+          </div>
+        </div>
+      </section>

+    <script>
+      // Navegació activa amb IntersectionObserver
+      const navLinks = document.querySelectorAll(".naveg-lat a");
+      const sectionObserver = new IntersectionObserver(
+        (entries) => {
+          entries.forEach((entry) => {
+            if (!entry.isIntersecting) return;
+            navLinks.forEach((l) => l.classList.remove("active"));
+            const match = document.querySelector(`.naveg-lat a[href="#${entry.target.id}"]`);
+            if (match) match.classList.add("active");
+          });
+        },
+        { threshold: 0.35 }
+      );
+      document.querySelectorAll("section[id]").forEach((s) => { 
+        if (s.id !== "projecte") sectionObserver.observe(s); 
+      });
+
+      // Eines extra
+      const toolsGrid = document.getElementById("toolsGrid");
+      if (toolsGrid) {
+        toolsGrid.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
+          const card = e.target.closest(".tool-card");
+          if (!card) return;
+          const tool = card.dataset.tool;
+          if (tool === "player") {
+            document.getElementById("playerModal").classList.add("open");
+            return;
+          }
+          toolsGrid.style.display = "none";
+          const panel = document.getElementById("panel-" + tool);
+          if (panel) panel.classList.add("active");
+        });
+      }
+
+      document.querySelectorAll("[data-back]").forEach((btn) => {
+        btn.addEventListener("click", () => {
+          btn.closest(".tool-panel")?.classList.remove("active");
+          toolsGrid.style.display = "";
+        });
+      });
+
+      // Generador QR
+      document.getElementById("qrBtn")?.addEventListener("click", () => {
+        const val = document.getElementById("qrInput").value.trim();
+        const out = document.getElementById("qrOutput");
+        if (!val) {
+          out.innerHTML = `<span style="color:var(--red);font-size:13px">Escriu alguna cosa primer</span>`;
+          return;
+        }
+        out.innerHTML = `<img src="https://api.qrserver.com/v1/create-qr-code/?size=200x200&data=${encodeURIComponent(val)}" alt="Codi QR">`;
+      });
+
+      // Calculadora
+      const calcScreen = document.getElementById("calcScreen");
+      document.querySelector(".calc-grid")?.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
+        const btn = e.target.closest(".calc-key");
+        if (!btn) return;
+        const { action, val } = btn.dataset;
+        if (action === "digit") calcScreen.value += val;
+        else if (action === "op")  calcScreen.value += val;
+        else if (action === "del") calcScreen.value = calcScreen.value.slice(0, -1);
+        else if (action === "clear") calcScreen.value = "";
+        else if (action === "eval") {
+          try { calcScreen.value = Function('"use strict";return (' + calcScreen.value + ")")(); }
+          catch { calcScreen.value = "Error"; }
+        }
+      });
+
+      document.addEventListener("keydown", (e) => {
+        if (!document.getElementById("panel-calc")?.classList.contains("active")) return;
+        if (/^[0-9.]$/.test(e.key))       calcScreen.value += e.key;
+        else if (["+", "-", "*", "/"].includes(e.key)) calcScreen.value += e.key;
+        else if (e.key === "Enter")        document.querySelector('[data-action="eval"]').click();
+        else if (e.key === "Backspace")    calcScreen.value = calcScreen.value.slice(0, -1);
+        else if (e.key === "Escape")       calcScreen.value = "";
+      });
+
+      // Modal reproductor
+      const playerModal = document.getElementById("playerModal");
+      document.getElementById("modalClose")?.addEventListener("click", () => { playerModal.classList.remove("open"); });
+      playerModal?.addEventListener("click", (e) => { if (e.target === playerModal) playerModal.classList.remove("open"); });
+
+      // Enllaç "El nostre projecte"
+      document.getElementById('link-projecte').addEventListener('click', function(e){
+        e.preventDefault();
+        document.querySelectorAll('main > section:not(#projecte)').forEach(s => s.style.display = 'none');
+        document.getElementById('projecte').style.display = 'block';
+        window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });
+      });
+
+      // Enllaços de la sidebar (tornar a la vista normal)
+      document.querySelectorAll('.naveg-lat a').forEach(a => {
+        a.addEventListener('click', function(e){
+          const id = this.getAttribute('href').slice(1);
+          if (document.getElementById(id) && id !== 'projecte') {
+            e.preventDefault();
+            document.getElementById('projecte').style.display = 'none';
+            document.querySelectorAll('main > section:not(#projecte)').forEach(s => s.style.display = 'block');
+            document.getElementById(id).scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });
+          }
+        });
+      });
+
+      document.querySelectorAll('.btn-outline').forEach(b => b.addEventListener('click', e => e.preventDefault()));
+    </script>
+  </body>
+</html>

+    <script>
+      const navLinks = document.querySelectorAll(".naveg-lat a");
+      const sectionObserver = new IntersectionObserver(
+        (entries) => {
+          entries.forEach((entry) => {
+            if (!entry.isIntersecting) return;
+            navLinks.forEach((l) => l.classList.remove("active"));
+            const match = document.querySelector(`.naveg-lat a[href="#${entry.target.id}"]`);
+            if (match) match.classList.add("active");
+          });
+        },
+        { threshold: 0.35 }
+      );
+      document.querySelectorAll("section[id]").forEach((s) => { sectionObserver.observe(s); });
+
+      const toolsGrid = document.getElementById("toolsGrid");
+      if (toolsGrid) {
+        toolsGrid.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
+          const card = e.target.closest(".tool-card");
+          if (!card) return;
+          const tool = card.dataset.tool;
+          if (tool === "player") {
+            document.getElementById("playerModal").classList.add("open");
+            return;
+          }
+          toolsGrid.style.display = "none";
+          const panel = document.getElementById("panel-" + tool);
+          if (panel) panel.classList.add("active");
+        });
+      }
+
+      document.querySelectorAll("[data-back]").forEach((btn) => {
+        btn.addEventListener("click", () => {
+          btn.closest(".tool-panel")?.classList.remove("active");
+          toolsGrid.style.display = "";
+        });
+      });
+
+      document.getElementById("qrBtn")?.addEventListener("click", () => {
+        const val = document.getElementById("qrInput").value.trim();
+        const out = document.getElementById("qrOutput");
+        if (!val) {
+          out.innerHTML = `<span style="color:var(--red);font-size:13px">Escriu alguna cosa primer</span>`;
+          return;
+        }
+        out.innerHTML = `<img src="https://api.qrserver.com/v1/create-qr-code/?size=200x200&data=${encodeURIComponent(val)}" alt="Codi QR">`;
+      });
+
+      const calcScreen = document.getElementById("calcScreen");
+      document.querySelector(".calc-grid")?.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
+        const btn = e.target.closest(".calc-key");
+        if (!btn) return;
+        const { action, val } = btn.dataset;
+        if (action === "digit") calcScreen.value += val;
+        else if (action === "op")  calcScreen.value += val;
+        else if (action === "del") calcScreen.value = calcScreen.value.slice(0, -1);
+        else if (action === "clear") calcScreen.value = "";
+        else if (action === "eval") {
+          try { calcScreen.value = Function('"use strict";return (' + calcScreen.value + ")")(); }
+          catch { calcScreen.value = "Error"; }
+        }
+      });
+
+      document.addEventListener("keydown", (e) => {
+        if (!document.getElementById("panel-calc")?.classList.contains("active")) return;
+        if (/^[0-9.]$/.test(e.key))       calcScreen.value += e.key;
+        else if (["+", "-", "*", "/"].includes(e.key)) calcScreen.value += e.key;
+        else if (e.key === "Enter")        document.querySelector('[data-action="eval"]').click();
+        else if (e.key === "Backspace")    calcScreen.value = calcScreen.value.slice(0, -1);
+        else if (e.key === "Escape")       calcScreen.value = "";
+      });
+
+      const playerModal = document.getElementById("playerModal");
+      document.getElementById("modalClose")?.addEventListener("click", () => { playerModal.classList.remove("open"); });
+      playerModal?.addEventListener("click", (e) => { if (e.target === playerModal) playerModal.classList.remove("open"); });
+
+      document.querySelectorAll('.btn-outline').forEach(b => b.addEventListener('click', e => e.preventDefault()));
+    </script>
+  </body>
+</html>
\ No newline at end of file

+    <script>
+      const sidebar = document.getElementById("sidebar");
+      const burgerBtn = document.getElementById("burgerBtn");
+
+      if (burgerBtn) {
+        burgerBtn.addEventListener("click", () => {
+          sidebar.classList.toggle("open");
+        });
+      }
+
+      document.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
+        if (!sidebar || !burgerBtn) return;
+        if (!sidebar.contains(e.target) && !burgerBtn.contains(e.target) && !e.target.closest('.mobile-bar')) {
+          sidebar.classList.remove("open");
+        }
+      });
+
+      const navLinks = document.querySelectorAll(".naveg-lat a");
+      const sectionObserver = new IntersectionObserver(
+        (entries) => {
+          entries.forEach((entry) => {
+            if (!entry.isIntersecting) return;
+            navLinks.forEach((l) => l.classList.remove("active"));
+            const match = document.querySelector(`.naveg-lat a[href="#${entry.target.id}"]`);
+            if (match) match.classList.add("active");
+          });
+        },
+        { threshold: 0.35 }
+      );
+      document.querySelectorAll("section[id]").forEach((s) => { sectionObserver.observe(s); });
+
+      const toolsGrid = document.getElementById("toolsGrid");
+      if (toolsGrid) {
+        toolsGrid.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
+          const card = e.target.closest(".tool-card");
+          if (!card) return;
+          const tool = card.dataset.tool;
+          if (tool === "player") {
+            document.getElementById("playerModal").classList.add("open");
+            return;
+          }
+          toolsGrid.style.display = "none";
+          const panel = document.getElementById("panel-" + tool);
+          if (panel) panel.classList.add("active");
+        });
+      }
+
+      document.querySelectorAll("[data-back]").forEach((btn) => {
+        btn.addEventListener("click", () => {
+          btn.closest(".tool-panel")?.classList.remove("active");
+          toolsGrid.style.display = "";
+        });
+      });
+
+      document.getElementById("qrBtn")?.addEventListener("click", () => {
+        const val = document.getElementById("qrInput").value.trim();
+        const out = document.getElementById("qrOutput");
+        if (!val) {
+          out.innerHTML = `<span style="color:var(--red);font-size:13px">Escriu alguna cosa primer</span>`;
+          return;
+        }
+        out.innerHTML = `<img src="https://api.qrserver.com/v1/create-qr-code/?size=200x200&data=${encodeURIComponent(val)}" alt="Codi QR" width="200" height="200">`;
+      });
+
+      const calcScreen = document.getElementById("calcScreen");
+      document.querySelector(".calc-grid")?.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
+        const btn = e.target.closest(".calc-key");
+        if (!btn) return;
+        const { action, val } = btn.dataset;
+        if (action === "digit") calcScreen.value += val;
+        else if (action === "op") calcScreen.value += val;
+        else if (action === "del") calcScreen.value = calcScreen.value.slice(0, -1);
+        else if (action === "clear") calcScreen.value = "";
+        else if (action === "eval") {
+          try { calcScreen.value = Function('"use strict";return (' + calcScreen.value + ")")(); }
+          catch { calcScreen.value = "Error"; }
+        }
+      });
+
+      document.addEventListener("keydown", (e) => {
+        if (!document.getElementById("panel-calc")?.classList.contains("active")) return;
+        if (/^[0-9.]$/.test(e.key)) calcScreen.value += e.key;
+        else if (["+", "-", "*", "/"].includes(e.key)) calcScreen.value += e.key;
+        else if (e.key === "Enter") document.querySelector('[data-action="eval"]').click();
+        else if (e.key === "Backspace") calcScreen.value = calcScreen.value.slice(0, -1);
+        else if (e.key === "Escape") calcScreen.value = "";
+      });
+
+      const playerModal = document.getElementById("playerModal");
+      document.getElementById("modalClose")?.addEventListener("click", () => { playerModal.classList.remove("open"); });
+      playerModal?.addEventListener("click", (e) => { if (e.target === playerModal) playerModal.classList.remove("open"); });
+
+      document.querySelectorAll('.btn-outline').forEach(b => b.addEventListener('click', e => e.preventDefault()));
+    </script>
+  </body>
+</html>

+
+    <script>
+      const sidebar = document.getElementById("sidebar");
+      const burgerBtn = document.getElementById("burgerBtn");
+
+      // Menu Mòbil
+      if (burgerBtn) {
+        burgerBtn.addEventListener("click", () => {
+          sidebar.classList.toggle("open");
+        });
+      }
+
+      // Quan fas clic fora del menú, es tanca automàticament
+      document.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
+        if (!sidebar || !burgerBtn) return;
+
+        if (!sidebar.contains(e.target) && !burgerBtn.contains(e.target)) {
+          sidebar.classList.remove("open");
+        }
+      });
+
+      // Nav Activa 
+      const navLinks = document.querySelectorAll(".naveg-lat a");
+
+      const sectionObserver = new IntersectionObserver(
+        (entries) => {
+          entries.forEach((entry) => {
+            if (!entry.isIntersecting) return;
+
+            navLinks.forEach((l) => l.classList.remove("active"));
+
+            const match = document.querySelector(
+              `.naveg-lat a[href="#${entry.target.id}"]`,
+            );
+
+            if (match) match.classList.add("active");
+          });
+        },
+        { threshold: 0.35 },
+      );
+
+      document.querySelectorAll("section[id]").forEach((s) => {
+        sectionObserver.observe(s);
+      });
+
+      // Eines extra
+      const toolsGrid = document.getElementById("toolsGrid");
+
+      if (toolsGrid) {
+        toolsGrid.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
+          const card = e.target.closest(".tool-card");
+          if (!card) return;
+
+          const tool = card.dataset.tool;
+
+          if (tool === "player") {
+            document.getElementById("playerModal").classList.add("open");
+            return;
+          }
+
+          const panel = document.getElementById("panel-" + tool);
+          if (panel) panel.classList.add("active");
+        });
+      }
+
+      // Volver atrás
+      document.querySelectorAll("[data-back]").forEach((btn) => {
+        btn.addEventListener("click", () => {
+          btn.closest(".tool-panel")?.classList.remove("active");
+        });
+      });
+
+      // QR
+      document.getElementById("qrBtn")?.addEventListener("click", () => {
+        const val = document.getElementById("qrInput").value.trim();
+        const out = document.getElementById("qrOutput");
+
+        if (!val) {
+          out.innerHTML = `<span style="color:red;font-size:13px">Escribe algo primero</span>`;
+          return;
+        }
+
+        out.innerHTML = `
+    <img src="https://api.qrserver.com/v1/create-qr-code/?size=200x200&data=${encodeURIComponent(val)}">
+  `;
+      });
+
+      // Calculadora 
+      const calcScreen = document.getElementById("calcScreen");
+
+      document.querySelector(".calc-grid")?.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
+        const btn = e.target.closest(".calc-key");
+        if (!btn) return;
+
+        const { action, val } = btn.dataset;
+
+        if (action === "digit") calcScreen.value += val;
+        else if (action === "op") calcScreen.value += val;
+        else if (action === "del")
+          calcScreen.value = calcScreen.value.slice(0, -1);
+        else if (action === "clear") calcScreen.value = "";
+        else if (action === "eval") {
+          try {
+            calcScreen.value = Function(
+              '"use strict";return (' + calcScreen.value + ")",
+            )();
+          } catch {
+            calcScreen.value = "Error";
+          }
+        }
+      });
+
+      // Calculadora (teclado)
+      document.addEventListener("keydown", (e) => {
+        if (
+          !document.getElementById("panel-calc")?.classList.contains("active")
+        )
+          return;
+
+        if (/^[0-9.]$/.test(e.key)) calcScreen.value += e.key;
+        else if (["+", "-", "*", "/"].includes(e.key))
+          calcScreen.value += e.key;
+        else if (e.key === "Enter")
+          document.querySelector('[data-action="eval"]').click();
+        else if (e.key === "Backspace")
+          calcScreen.value = calcScreen.value.slice(0, -1);
+        else if (e.key === "Escape") calcScreen.value = "";
+      });
+
+      // Reproductor
+      const playerModal = document.getElementById("playerModal");
+      document.getElementById("modalClose")?.addEventListener("click", () => {
+        playerModal.classList.remove("open");
+      });
+
+      playerModal?.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
+        if (e.target === playerModal) {
+          playerModal.classList.remove("open");
+        }
+      });
+    </script>
+  </body>
+</html>

-
-        if (!sidebar.contains(e.target) && !burgerBtn.contains(e.target)) {
-          sidebar.classList.remove("open");

-      // Nav Activa

-
-      const sectionObserver = new IntersectionObserver(
-        (entries) => {
-          entries.forEach((entry) => {
-            if (!entry.isIntersecting) return;
-
-            navLinks.forEach((l) => l.classList.remove("active"));
-
-            const match = document.querySelector(
-              `.naveg-lat a[href="#${entry.target.id}"]`,
-            );
-
-            if (match) match.classList.add("active");
-          });
-        },
-        { threshold: 0.35 },
-      );
-
-      document.querySelectorAll("section[id]").forEach((s) => {
-        sectionObserver.observe(s);
-      });
-
-      // Eines extra
-      const toolsGrid = document.getElementById("toolsGrid");
-
-      if (toolsGrid) {

-            document.getElementById("playerModal").classList.add("open");
-            return;
-          }
-
-          const panel = document.getElementById("panel-" + tool);
-          if (panel) panel.classList.add("active");
-        });

-
-      // Volver atrás
-      document.querySelectorAll("[data-back]").forEach((btn) => {

-          btn.closest(".tool-panel")?.classList.remove("active");
-        });

-      // QR

-        if (action === "digit") calcScreen.value += val;

-          calcScreen.value = calcScreen.value.slice(0, -1);

-            )();

-            calcScreen.value = "Error";
-          }

-      // Calculadora (teclado)

-        else if (e.key === "Backspace")
-          calcScreen.value = calcScreen.value.slice(0, -1);

+<script>
+const sidebar=document.getElementById('sidebar');
+const burgerBtn=document.getElementById('burgerBtn');
+
+if(burgerBtn){
+burgerBtn.addEventListener('click',()=>{
+sidebar.classList.toggle('open');
+});
+}
+
+document.addEventListener('click',(e)=>{
+if(!sidebar||!burgerBtn) return;
+if(!sidebar.contains(e.target)&&!burgerBtn.contains(e.target)){
+sidebar.classList.remove('open');
+}
+});
+
+const navLinks=document.querySelectorAll('.naveg-lat a');
+
+const sectionObserver=new IntersectionObserver((entries)=>{
+entries.forEach(entry=>{
+if(!entry.isIntersecting) return;
+navLinks.forEach(l=>l.classList.remove('active'));
+const match=document.querySelector(`.naveg-lat a[href="#${entry.target.id}"]`);
+if(match) match.classList.add('active');
+});
+},{threshold:.35});
+
+document.querySelectorAll('section[id]').forEach(s=>{
+sectionObserver.observe(s);
+});
+
+const toolsGrid=document.getElementById('toolsGrid');
+
+if(toolsGrid){
+toolsGrid.addEventListener('click',(e)=>{
+const card=e.target.closest('.tool-card');
+if(!card) return;
+
+const tool=card.dataset.tool;
+
+if(tool==='player'){
+document.getElementById('playerModal').classList.add('open');
+return;
+}
+
+document.getElementById('panel-'+tool)?.classList.add('active');
+});
+}
+
+// Tornar enrere
+
+document.querySelectorAll('[data-back]').forEach(btn=>{
+btn.addEventListener('click',()=>{
+btn.closest('.tool-panel')?.classList.remove('active');
+});
+});
+
+// QR
+
+document.getElementById('qrBtn')?.addEventListener('click',()=>{
+const val=document.getElementById('qrInput').value.trim();
+const out=document.getElementById('qrOutput');
+
+if(!val){
+out.innerHTML=`<span style="color:red;font-size:13px">Escriu alguna cosa primer</span>`;
+return;
+}
+
+out.innerHTML=`
+<img src="https://api.qrserver.com/v1/create-qr-code/?size=200x200&data=${encodeURIComponent(val)}">
+`;
+});
+
+const calcScreen=document.getElementById('calcScreen');
+
+document.querySelector('.calc-grid')?.addEventListener('click',(e)=>{
+const btn=e.target.closest('.calc-key');
+if(!btn) return;
+
+const {action,val}=btn.dataset;
+
+if(action==='digit') calcScreen.value+=val;
+else if(action==='op') calcScreen.value+=val;
+else if(action==='del') calcScreen.value=calcScreen.value.slice(0,-1);
+else if(action==='clear') calcScreen.value='';
+else if(action==='eval'){
+try{
+calcScreen.value=Function(
+'"use strict";return ('+calcScreen.value+')'
+)();
+}catch{
+calcScreen.value='Error';
+}
+}
+});
+
+const playerModal=document.getElementById('playerModal');
+document.getElementById('modalClose')?.addEventListener('click',()=>{
+playerModal.classList.remove('open');
+});
+
+playerModal?.addEventListener('click',(e)=>{
+if(e.target===playerModal){
+playerModal.classList.remove('open');
+}
+});
+</script>
+
+</body>

+    <script>

+
+      // ===== MENU MÓVIL =====
+      if (burgerBtn) {
+        burgerBtn.addEventListener("click", () => {
+          sidebar.classList.toggle("open");
+        });
+      }
+
+      // cerrar al hacer click fuera
+      document.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
+        if (!sidebar || !burgerBtn) return;
+
+        if (!sidebar.contains(e.target) && !burgerBtn.contains(e.target)) {

+

+          entries.forEach((entry) => {
+            if (!entry.isIntersecting) return;
+
+            navLinks.forEach((l) => l.classList.remove("active"));
+
+            const match = document.querySelector(
+              `.sidebar-nav a[href="#${entry.target.id}"]`,

+
+      // ===== TOOLS =====
+      const toolsGrid = document.getElementById("toolsGrid");
+
+      if (toolsGrid) {
+        toolsGrid.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
+          const card = e.target.closest(".tool-card");
+          if (!card) return;
+
+          const tool = card.dataset.tool;
+
+          if (tool === "player") {
+            document.getElementById("playerModal").classList.add("open");
+            return;
+          }
+
+          const panel = document.getElementById("panel-" + tool);
+          if (panel) panel.classList.add("active");
+        });
+      }
+
+      // volver atrás
+      document.querySelectorAll("[data-back]").forEach((btn) => {
+        btn.addEventListener("click", () => {
+          btn.closest(".tool-panel")?.classList.remove("active");
+        });
+      });
+
+      // ===== QR =====
+      document.getElementById("qrBtn")?.addEventListener("click", () => {
+        const val = document.getElementById("qrInput").value.trim();
+        const out = document.getElementById("qrOutput");
+
+        if (!val) {
+          out.innerHTML = `<span style="color:red;font-size:13px">Escribe algo primero</span>`;
+          return;
+        }
+
+        out.innerHTML = `
     <img src="https://api.qrserver.com/v1/create-qr-code/?size=200x200&data=${encodeURIComponent(val)}">
   `;
-});
-
-// ===== CALCULADORA =====
-const calcScreen = document.getElementById('calcScreen');
-

+      });
+
+      // ===== CALCULADORA =====
+      const calcScreen = document.getElementById("calcScreen");
+
+      document.querySelector(".calc-grid")?.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
+        const btn = e.target.closest(".calc-key");
+        if (!btn) return;
+
+        const { action, val } = btn.dataset;
+
+        if (action === "digit") calcScreen.value += val;
+        else if (action === "op") calcScreen.value += val;
+        else if (action === "del")
+          calcScreen.value = calcScreen.value.slice(0, -1);
+        else if (action === "clear") calcScreen.value = "";
+        else if (action === "eval") {
+          try {
+            calcScreen.value = Function(
+              '"use strict";return (' + calcScreen.value + ")",
+            )();
+          } catch {
+            calcScreen.value = "Error";
+          }
+        }
+      });
+
+      // teclado
+      document.addEventListener("keydown", (e) => {
+        if (
+          !document.getElementById("panel-calc")?.classList.contains("active")
+        )
+          return;
+
+        if (/^[0-9.]$/.test(e.key)) calcScreen.value += e.key;
+        else if (["+", "-", "*", "/"].includes(e.key))
+          calcScreen.value += e.key;
+        else if (e.key === "Enter")
+          document.querySelector('[data-action="eval"]').click();
+        else if (e.key === "Backspace")
+          calcScreen.value = calcScreen.value.slice(0, -1);
+        else if (e.key === "Escape") calcScreen.value = "";
+      });
+
+      // ===== MODAL =====
+      const playerModal = document.getElementById("playerModal");
+      document.getElementById("modalClose")?.addEventListener("click", () => {
+        playerModal.classList.remove("open");
+      });
+
+      playerModal?.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
+        if (e.target === playerModal) {
+          playerModal.classList.remove("open");
+        }
+      });
+    </script>
+  </body>
 </html>

+  <script>
+    // Sidebar mobile
+    const sidebar   = document.getElementById('sidebar');
+    const burgerBtn = document.getElementById('burgerBtn');
+
+    burgerBtn.addEventListener('click', () => sidebar.classList.toggle('open'));
+
+    document.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
+      if (!sidebar.contains(e.target) && !burgerBtn.contains(e.target)) {
+        sidebar.classList.remove('open');
+      }
+    });
+
+    // Nav actiu en scroll
+    const navLinks = document.querySelectorAll('.sidebar-nav a');
+
+    const sectionObserver = new IntersectionObserver((entries) => {
+      entries.forEach(entry => {
+        if (!entry.isIntersecting) return;
+        navLinks.forEach(l => l.classList.remove('active'));
+        const match = document.querySelector(`.sidebar-nav a[href="#${entry.target.id}"]`);
+        if (match) match.classList.add('active');
+      });
+    }, { threshold: 0.35 });
+
+    document.querySelectorAll('section[id]').forEach(s => sectionObserver.observe(s));
+
+    // Eines
+    document.getElementById('toolsGrid').addEventListener('click', (e) => {
+      const card = e.target.closest('.tool-card');
+      if (!card) return;
+      const tool = card.dataset.tool;
+
+      if (tool === 'player') {
+        document.getElementById('playerModal').classList.add('open');
+        return;
+      }
+
+      const panel = document.getElementById('panel-' + tool);
+      if (panel) panel.classList.add('active');
+    });
+
+    document.querySelectorAll('[data-back]').forEach(btn => {
+      btn.addEventListener('click', () => btn.closest('.tool-panel').classList.remove('active'));
+    });
+
+    // QR
+    document.getElementById('qrBtn').addEventListener('click', () => {
+      const val = document.getElementById('qrInput').value.trim();
+      const out = document.getElementById('qrOutput');
+
+      if (!val) {
+        out.innerHTML = '<span style="color:var(--red);font-size:13px">Introdueix un URL o text primer</span>';
+        return;
+      }
+
+      out.innerHTML = `<img src="https://api.qrserver.com/v1/create-qr-code/?size=200x200&data=${encodeURIComponent(val)}" alt="Codi QR" width="200" height="200">`;
+    });
+
+    // Calculadora
+    const calcScreen = document.getElementById('calcScreen');
+
+    document.querySelector('.calc-grid').addEventListener('click', (e) => {
+      const btn = e.target.closest('.calc-key');
+      if (!btn) return;
+      const { action, val } = btn.dataset;
+
+      if      (action === 'digit') calcScreen.value += val;
+      else if (action === 'op')    calcScreen.value += val;
+      else if (action === 'del')   calcScreen.value = calcScreen.value.slice(0, -1);
+      else if (action === 'clear') calcScreen.value = '';
+      else if (action === 'eval') {
+        try {
+          calcScreen.value = Function('"use strict"; return (' + calcScreen.value + ')')();
+        } catch {
+          calcScreen.value = 'Error';
+        }
+      }
+    });
+
+    document.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
+      if (!document.getElementById('panel-calc').classList.contains('active')) return;
+      if (/^[0-9.]$/.test(e.key))                calcScreen.value += e.key;
+      else if (['+','-','*','/'].includes(e.key)) calcScreen.value += e.key;
+      else if (e.key === 'Enter')                 document.querySelector('[data-action="eval"]').click();
+      else if (e.key === 'Backspace')             calcScreen.value = calcScreen.value.slice(0, -1);
+      else if (e.key === 'Escape')                calcScreen.value = '';
+    });
+
+    // Modal
+    const playerModal = document.getElementById('playerModal');
+    document.getElementById('modalClose').addEventListener('click', () => playerModal.classList.remove('open'));
+    playerModal.addEventListener('click', (e) => { if (e.target === playerModal) playerModal.classList.remove('open'); });
+  </script>
+</body>
+</html>

+  <script>
+    // Sidebar mobile
+    const sidebar   = document.getElementById('sidebar');
+    const burgerBtn = document.getElementById('burgerBtn');
+
+    burgerBtn.addEventListener('click', () => sidebar.classList.toggle('open'));
+
+    document.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
+      if (!sidebar.contains(e.target) && !burgerBtn.contains(e.target)) {
+        sidebar.classList.remove('open');
+      }
+    });
+
+    // Nav actiu en scroll
+    const navLinks = document.querySelectorAll('.sidebar-nav a');
+
+    const sectionObserver = new IntersectionObserver((entries) => {
+      entries.forEach(entry => {
+        if (!entry.isIntersecting) return;
+        navLinks.forEach(l => l.classList.remove('active'));
+        const match = document.querySelector(`.sidebar-nav a[href="#${entry.target.id}"]`);
+        if (match) match.classList.add('active');
+      });
+    }, { threshold: 0.35 });
+
+    document.querySelectorAll('section[id]').forEach(s => sectionObserver.observe(s));
+
+    // Eines
+    document.getElementById('toolsGrid').addEventListener('click', (e) => {
+      const card = e.target.closest('.tool-card');
+      if (!card) return;
+      const tool = card.dataset.tool;
+
+      if (tool === 'player') {
+        document.getElementById('playerModal').classList.add('open');
+        return;
+      }
+
+      const panel = document.getElementById('panel-' + tool);
+      if (panel) panel.classList.add('active');
+    });
+
+    document.querySelectorAll('[data-back]').forEach(btn => {
+      btn.addEventListener('click', () => btn.closest('.tool-panel').classList.remove('active'));
+    });
+
+    // QR
+    document.getElementById('qrBtn').addEventListener('click', () => {
+      const val = document.getElementById('qrInput').value.trim();
+      const out = document.getElementById('qrOutput');
+
+      if (!val) {
+        out.innerHTML = '<span style="color:var(--red);font-size:13px">Introdueix un URL o text primer</span>';
+        return;
+      }
+
+      out.innerHTML = `<img src="https://api.qrserver.com/v1/create-qr-code/?size=200x200&data=${encodeURIComponent(val)}" alt="Codi QR" width="200" height="200">`;
+    });
+
+    // Calculadora
+    const calcScreen = document.getElementById('calcScreen');
+
+    document.querySelector('.calc-grid').addEventListener('click', (e) => {
+      const btn = e.target.closest('.calc-key');
+      if (!btn) return;
+      const { action, val } = btn.dataset;
+
+      if      (action === 'digit') calcScreen.value += val;
+      else if (action === 'op')    calcScreen.value += val;
+      else if (action === 'del')   calcScreen.value = calcScreen.value.slice(0, -1);
+      else if (action === 'clear') calcScreen.value = '';
+      else if (action === 'eval') {
+        try {
+          calcScreen.value = Function('"use strict"; return (' + calcScreen.value + ')')();
+        } catch {
+          calcScreen.value = 'Error';
+        }
+      }
+    });
+
+    document.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
+      if (!document.getElementById('panel-calc').classList.contains('active')) return;
+      if (/^[0-9.]$/.test(e.key))                calcScreen.value += e.key;
+      else if (['+','-','*','/'].includes(e.key)) calcScreen.value += e.key;
+      else if (e.key === 'Enter')                 document.querySelector('[data-action="eval"]').click();
+      else if (e.key === 'Backspace')             calcScreen.value = calcScreen.value.slice(0, -1);
+      else if (e.key === 'Escape')                calcScreen.value = '';
+    });
+
+    // Modal
+    const playerModal = document.getElementById('playerModal');
+    document.getElementById('modalClose').addEventListener('click', () => playerModal.classList.remove('open'));
+    playerModal.addEventListener('click', (e) => { if (e.target === playerModal) playerModal.classList.remove('open'); });
+  </script>
+</body>
+</html>

+    const sidebar = document.getElementById('sidebar');
+const burgerBtn = document.getElementById('burgerBtn');
+
+// ===== MENU MÓVIL =====
+if (burgerBtn) {
+  burgerBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
+    sidebar.classList.toggle('open');
+  });
+}
+
+// cerrar al hacer click fuera
+document.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
+  if (!sidebar || !burgerBtn) return;
+
+  if (!sidebar.contains(e.target) && !burgerBtn.contains(e.target)) {
+    sidebar.classList.remove('open');
+  }
+});
+
+// ===== NAV ACTIVO =====
+const navLinks = document.querySelectorAll('.sidebar-nav a');
+
+const sectionObserver = new IntersectionObserver((entries) => {
+  entries.forEach(entry => {
+    if (!entry.isIntersecting) return;
+
+    navLinks.forEach(l => l.classList.remove('active'));
+
+    const match = document.querySelector(
+      `.sidebar-nav a[href="#${entry.target.id}"]`
+    );
+
+    if (match) match.classList.add('active');
+  });
+}, { threshold: 0.35 });
+
+document.querySelectorAll('section[id]').forEach(s => {
+  sectionObserver.observe(s);
+});
+
+// ===== TOOLS =====
+const toolsGrid = document.getElementById('toolsGrid');
+
+if (toolsGrid) {
+  toolsGrid.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
+    const card = e.target.closest('.tool-card');
+    if (!card) return;
+
+    const tool = card.dataset.tool;
+
+    if (tool === 'player') {
+      document.getElementById('playerModal').classList.add('open');
+      return;
+    }
+
+    const panel = document.getElementById('panel-' + tool);
+    if (panel) panel.classList.add('active');
+  });
+}
+
+// volver atrás
+document.querySelectorAll('[data-back]').forEach(btn => {
+  btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
+    btn.closest('.tool-panel')?.classList.remove('active');
+  });
+});
+
+// ===== QR =====
+document.getElementById('qrBtn')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
+  const val = document.getElementById('qrInput').value.trim();
+  const out = document.getElementById('qrOutput');
+
+  if (!val) {
+    out.innerHTML = `<span style="color:red;font-size:13px">Escribe algo primero</span>`;
+    return;
+  }
+
+  out.innerHTML = `
+    <img src="https://api.qrserver.com/v1/create-qr-code/?size=200x200&data=${encodeURIComponent(val)}">
+  `;
+});
+
+// ===== CALCULADORA =====
+const calcScreen = document.getElementById('calcScreen');
+
+document.querySelector('.calc-grid')?.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
+  const btn = e.target.closest('.calc-key');
+  if (!btn) return;
+
+  const { action, val } = btn.dataset;
+
+  if (action === 'digit') calcScreen.value += val;
+  else if (action === 'op') calcScreen.value += val;
+  else if (action === 'del') calcScreen.value = calcScreen.value.slice(0, -1);
+  else if (action === 'clear') calcScreen.value = '';
+  else if (action === 'eval') {
+    try {
+      calcScreen.value = Function('"use strict";return (' + calcScreen.value + ')')();
+    } catch {
+      calcScreen.value = 'Error';
+    }
+  }
+});
+
+// teclado
+document.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
+  if (!document.getElementById('panel-calc')?.classList.contains('active')) return;
+
+  if (/^[0-9.]$/.test(e.key)) calcScreen.value += e.key;
+  else if (['+','-','*','/'].includes(e.key)) calcScreen.value += e.key;
+  else if (e.key === 'Enter') document.querySelector('[data-action="eval"]').click();
+  else if (e.key === 'Backspace') calcScreen.value = calcScreen.value.slice(0, -1);
+  else if (e.key === 'Escape') calcScreen.value = '';
+});
+
+// ===== MODAL =====
+const playerModal = document.getElementById('playerModal');
+document.getElementById('modalClose')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
+  playerModal.classList.remove('open');
+});
+
+playerModal?.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
+  if (e.target === playerModal) {
+    playerModal.classList.remove('open');
+  }
+});
   </script>

+  <script>
+    // Sidebar mobile
+    const sidebar   = document.getElementById('sidebar');
+    const burgerBtn = document.getElementById('burgerBtn');
+
+    burgerBtn.addEventListener('click', () => sidebar.classList.toggle('open'));
+
+    document.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
+      if (!sidebar.contains(e.target) && !burgerBtn.contains(e.target)) {
+        sidebar.classList.remove('open');
+      }
+    });
+
+    // Nav actiu en scroll
+    const navLinks = document.querySelectorAll('.sidebar-nav a');
+
+    const sectionObserver = new IntersectionObserver((entries) => {
+      entries.forEach(entry => {
+        if (!entry.isIntersecting) return;
+        navLinks.forEach(l => l.classList.remove('active'));
+        const match = document.querySelector(`.sidebar-nav a[href="#${entry.target.id}"]`);
+        if (match) match.classList.add('active');
+      });
+    }, { threshold: 0.35 });
+
+    document.querySelectorAll('section[id]').forEach(s => sectionObserver.observe(s));
+
+    // Eines
+    document.getElementById('toolsGrid').addEventListener('click', (e) => {
+      const card = e.target.closest('.tool-card');
+      if (!card) return;
+      const tool = card.dataset.tool;
+
+      if (tool === 'player') {
+        document.getElementById('playerModal').classList.add('open');
+        return;
+      }
+
+      const panel = document.getElementById('panel-' + tool);
+      if (panel) panel.classList.add('active');
+    });
+
+    document.querySelectorAll('[data-back]').forEach(btn => {
+      btn.addEventListener('click', () => btn.closest('.tool-panel').classList.remove('active'));
+    });
+
+    // QR
+    document.getElementById('qrBtn').addEventListener('click', () => {
+      const val = document.getElementById('qrInput').value.trim();
+      const out = document.getElementById('qrOutput');
+
+      if (!val) {
+        out.innerHTML = '<span style="color:var(--red);font-size:13px">Introdueix un URL o text primer</span>';
+        return;
+      }
+
+      out.innerHTML = `<img src="https://api.qrserver.com/v1/create-qr-code/?size=200x200&data=${encodeURIComponent(val)}" alt="Codi QR" width="200" height="200">`;
+    });
+
+    // Calculadora
+    const calcScreen = document.getElementById('calcScreen');
+
+    document.querySelector('.calc-grid').addEventListener('click', (e) => {
+      const btn = e.target.closest('.calc-key');
+      if (!btn) return;
+      const { action, val } = btn.dataset;
+
+      if      (action === 'digit') calcScreen.value += val;
+      else if (action === 'op')    calcScreen.value += val;
+      else if (action === 'del')   calcScreen.value = calcScreen.value.slice(0, -1);
+      else if (action === 'clear') calcScreen.value = '';
+      else if (action === 'eval') {
+        try {
+          calcScreen.value = Function('"use strict"; return (' + calcScreen.value + ')')();
+        } catch {
+          calcScreen.value = 'Error';
+        }
+      }
+    });
+
+    document.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
+      if (!document.getElementById('panel-calc').classList.contains('active')) return;
+      if (/^[0-9.]$/.test(e.key))                calcScreen.value += e.key;
+      else if (['+','-','*','/'].includes(e.key)) calcScreen.value += e.key;
+      else if (e.key === 'Enter')                 document.querySelector('[data-action="eval"]').click();
+      else if (e.key === 'Backspace')             calcScreen.value = calcScreen.value.slice(0, -1);
+      else if (e.key === 'Escape')                calcScreen.value = '';
+    });
+
+    // Modal
+    const playerModal = document.getElementById('playerModal');
+    document.getElementById('modalClose').addEventListener('click', () => playerModal.classList.remove('open'));
+    playerModal.addEventListener('click', (e) => { if (e.target === playerModal) playerModal.classList.remove('open'); });
+  </script>
+</body>
+</html>

+      border-radius: var(--radius);
+      cursor: pointer;
+      background: rgba(255,255,255,0.08);
+      color: var(--white);
+      transition: background 0.1s;
+    }
+
+    .calc-key:hover        { background: rgba(255,255,255,0.15); }
+    .calc-key.op           { background: var(--red); }
+    .calc-key.op:hover     { background: var(--red-dark); }
+    .calc-key.clear        { background: rgba(255,80,60,0.26); }
+    .calc-key.clear:hover  { background: rgba(255,80,60,0.42); }
+    .calc-key.equal        { background: #2a9d8f; grid-column: span 2; }
+    .calc-key.equal:hover  { background: #218f82; }

+
+  <script>
+    // Sidebar mobile
+    const sidebar   = document.getElementById('sidebar');
+    const burgerBtn = document.getElementById('burgerBtn');
+
+    burgerBtn.addEventListener('click', () => sidebar.classList.toggle('open'));
+
+    document.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
+      if (!sidebar.contains(e.target) && !burgerBtn.contains(e.target)) {
+        sidebar.classList.remove('open');
+      }
+    });
+
+    // Nav actiu en scroll
+    const navLinks = document.querySelectorAll('.sidebar-nav a');
+
+    const sectionObserver = new IntersectionObserver((entries) => {
+      entries.forEach(entry => {
+        if (!entry.isIntersecting) return;
+        navLinks.forEach(l => l.classList.remove('active'));
+        const match = document.querySelector(`.sidebar-nav a[href="#${entry.target.id}"]`);
+        if (match) match.classList.add('active');
+      });
+    }, { threshold: 0.35 });
+
+    document.querySelectorAll('section[id]').forEach(s => sectionObserver.observe(s));
+
+    // Eines
+    document.getElementById('toolsGrid').addEventListener('click', (e) => {
+      const card = e.target.closest('.tool-card');
+      if (!card) return;
+      const tool = card.dataset.tool;
+
+      if (tool === 'player') {
+        document.getElementById('playerModal').classList.add('open');
+        return;
+      }
+
+      const panel = document.getElementById('panel-' + tool);
+      if (panel) panel.classList.add('active');
+    });
+
+    document.querySelectorAll('[data-back]').forEach(btn => {
+      btn.addEventListener('click', () => btn.closest('.tool-panel').classList.remove('active'));
+    });
+
+    // QR
+    document.getElementById('qrBtn').addEventListener('click', () => {
+      const val = document.getElementById('qrInput').value.trim();
+      const out = document.getElementById('qrOutput');
+
+      if (!val) {
+        out.innerHTML = '<span style="color:var(--red);font-size:13px">Introdueix un URL o text primer</span>';
+        return;
+      }
+
+      out.innerHTML = `<img src="https://api.qrserver.com/v1/create-qr-code/?size=200x200&data=${encodeURIComponent(val)}" alt="Codi QR" width="200" height="200">`;
+    });
+
+    // Calculadora
+    const calcScreen = document.getElementById('calcScreen');
+
+    document.querySelector('.calc-grid').addEventListener('click', (e) => {
+      const btn = e.target.closest('.calc-key');
+      if (!btn) return;
+      const { action, val } = btn.dataset;
+
+      if      (action === 'digit') calcScreen.value += val;
+      else if (action === 'op')    calcScreen.value += val;
+      else if (action === 'del')   calcScreen.value = calcScreen.value.slice(0, -1);
+      else if (action === 'clear') calcScreen.value = '';
+      else if (action === 'eval') {
+        try {
+          calcScreen.value = Function('"use strict"; return (' + calcScreen.value + ')')();
+        } catch {
+          calcScreen.value = 'Error';
+        }
+      }
+    });
+
+    document.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
+      if (!document.getElementById('panel-calc').classList.contains('active')) return;
+      if (/^[0-9.]$/.test(e.key))                calcScreen.value += e.key;
+      else if (['+','-','*','/'].includes(e.key)) calcScreen.value += e.key;
+      else if (e.key === 'Enter')                 document.querySelector('[data-action="eval"]').click();
+      else if (e.key === 'Backspace')             calcScreen.value = calcScreen.value.slice(0, -1);
+      else if (e.key === 'Escape')                calcScreen.value = '';
+    });
+
+    // Modal
+    const playerModal = document.getElementById('playerModal');
+    document.getElementById('modalClose').addEventListener('click', () => playerModal.classList.remove('open'));
+    playerModal.addEventListener('click', (e) => { if (e.target === playerModal) playerModal.classList.remove('open'); });
+  </script>
+</body>
+</html>
